                                                                                            Annual Report 2018/19 年報                     1
CORPORATE PROFILE
企業簡介
Established in 1991, Luk Fook Holdings (International)
Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively
referred to as the “Group”) is one of the leading jewellery
retailers in Hong Kong and Mainland China. The Group principally
engages in the sourcing, designing, wholesaling, trademark
licensing and retailing of a variety of gold and platinum jewellery
and gem-set jewellery products, with a total of over 1,800
Lukfook Jewellery shops in 10 countries and regions across Hong
Kong, Macau, Mainland China, Singapore, Malaysia, Cambodia,
the Philippines, the United States, Canada and Australia.

The Company was listed on the Main Board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited in May 1997.

六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本
集團」）成立於1991年，是香港及中國內地主要珠寶零售商之一。
本集團主要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品之採購、設計、
批發、商標授權及零售業務。集團現時在香港、澳門、中國內地、
新加坡、馬來西亞、柬埔寨、菲律賓、美國、加拿大及澳洲共10個
國家和地區擁有逾1,800間六福珠寶零售店。

本公司於1997年5月在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。


CORPORATE VISION
企業願景
The corporate vision of the Group is to be the “Brand of Hong
Kong, Sparkling the World”. This is also our core brand value. The
Group always puts forth maximum effort for Lukfook to reach its
goal of becoming a globally recognised and desired brand, and
as one of the Hong Kong’s most renowned brands in the luxury
industry, the Group commits to crafting the finest jewellery for
customers to further establish its reputation in the international
market.

「香港名牌•國際演繹」為本集團的企業願景及品牌核心價值。
 本集團一直堅持不懈，盡心竭力使六福成為全球認知及喜愛的
 品牌。作為香港知名奢侈品牌，本集團致力為全球顧客提供優質
 首飾，以進一步確立其於國際市場上的聲譽。

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告

OUTLOOK                                                                 前景
During the Year under review, overall profit still recorded growth      於回顧年度內，儘管下半財年市場氣氛受中美
despite that the market sentiment was suddenly worsened under           貿易戰的不良影響突然轉差，猶幸整體利潤仍
the adverse impact of the US-China trade war in the second              有增長。惟本集團於香港及澳門市場之同店銷
half of the financial year. However, SSSG in the Hong Kong and          售 增 長 在2019年4月 至6月 首 三 週 期 間 錄 得 低
Macau markets of the Group recorded a low double-digit drop             雙位數字跌幅，而中國內地市場的自營店及品
for the period from April to the first 3 weeks of June 2019, while      牌店之同店銷售分別為中單位數字跌幅及低單
same store sales for Mainland China’s were a mid single-digit           位數字升幅。有鑑於中美貿易戰對宏觀經濟環
drop and a low single-digit growth for self-operated and licensed       境影響的持續不確定性及上半財年之高基數
shops respectively. With the continuing uncertainties on the            效應與下半財年的低基數，本集團因此預期於
macro-economic environment arising from the US-China trade              2019/20財年的業務表現將會維持於平穩狀態，
war, together with the high base effect in the first half and low       並期望得以持盈保泰。由於中國內地仍為深厚
base in the second half of the financial year, therefore, the Group     增長潛力所在，本集團對於中長線業務前景仍

TO CUSTOMERS: SIX HEARTFELT SERVICES                                對顧客：六心服務
A piece of jewellery could well be a piece of valuable memory       對許多顧客而言，珠寶的珍貴價值在於其背後
passing down through generations to many of our customers.          傳承的意義。本著「匠心獨運、服務為本、以客
In the spirit of “Exquisite Craftsmanship, Quality Services and     為先」的精神，我們致力以可持續發展和負責
Customer Orientation”, we are committed to managing and             任的方式營運和管理，從原材料採購、生產以
operating in a sustainable and responsible manner, being            至產品及服務責任均一絲不苟。
meticulous from raw material procurement, production to product
and service responsibilities.

Ethical Sourcing                                                    道德採購
In order to ensure that our jewellery products meet the relevant    為確保本集團所銷售的珠寶產品符合有關標
standards, we use quality raw materials from the procurement        準，我們由採購開始採用優質原材料。為確保
stage. To guarantee the source of raw materials such as             鑽石、黃金、翡翠玉石等原材料來源合法及優
diamonds, gold and jadeite are legal and of high quality, we have   質，我們採購自如上海黃金交易所及上海鑽石
purchased from industry-recognised suppliers with integrity such    交易所的會員及全球最大鑽石供應商De Beers
as members of the Shanghai Gold Exchange and Shanghai               等獲業界認可的誠信供應商。其中，De Beers通
Diamond Exchange and De Beers, the world’s largest diamond          過金伯利流程認證計劃，確保所供應的鑽石毛
supplier. Among them, De Beers has passed the Kimberley             胚並非「衝突鑽石」。
Process Certification Scheme, ensuring that the rough diamond
supplied are not “conflict diamonds”.

In addition, we enhance customer confidence through various         此外，我們通過各個產品品質認證計劃，加強
product quality certification programmes.                           顧客信心。



                                    By participating in the “Natural Jade Logo” programme jointly organised by the
                                    Hong Kong Jewellers’ and Goldsmiths’ Association and the Kowloon Pearls,
                                    Precious Stones, Jade, Gold and Silver Ornament Merchants Association, we
                                    ensure that our jade products are all untreated natural jade.
                                    通過參與由香港珠石玉器金銀首飾業商會與九龍珠石玉器金銀首飾業商會合

                               As stated in the Employee Handbook, employees are assessed annually and
                               given reasonable returns based on individual performance and contribution to the
                               development of the Group. The management will also review and approve the
                               remuneration policy on a regular basis with reference to similar market levels to
                               ensure fair returns and remuneration for employees.

環境、社會及管治報告

Continuation of the Story of Beauty


延續美麗故事

The jewellery industry is a traditional industry. It is an industry that combines creativity, craftsmanship and beauty.
Every piece of jewellery carries its own unique story and personality. In the past, it was the actual work experience
that counted. Therefore, many senior employees who have no academic qualifications started their career from
junior positions. However, with the progress of time, the Group’s branches have increased day by day, attracting
younger generations to enter the industry. In addition to retail experience, it has also begun to pursue academic
qualifications. At the same time, jewellery is a luxury consumer goods. Customers expect to buy products with
guaranteed quality. Therefore, customers have very strict requirements on quality standards and process quality.
We expect sales staff to introduce products in a detailed, careful and patient manner. In addition, in recent years,
growing attention has been paid to consumer rights. Good customer service and offering professional jewellery
knowledge to customers are often the key to success.

As a result, the Group has always attached great importance to staff development by provision of different types
of training courses, such as orientation classes, product knowledge, customer service, shop management and
service management workshops, jewellery knowledge courses, and other courses for staff. Mainland China staff
can also log in and attend online training through the “Jewellery V Course” mobile application. Through professional
training, we ensure that our employees are professional and adaptable, allowing them to broaden their horizons.


珠寶行業是一個傳統行業，可說是揉合創意、工藝及美感的行業，每一件珠寶首
飾，都有它獨特的故事和個性。以往講求是店舖內之實際工作經驗，故很多資深
員工，都沒有甚麼學歷，可說是紅褲子出身。但隨著時代進步，本集團分店與日
俱增，吸引了年青一輩入行，除零售經驗外，亦開始講求學歷。同時，珠寶首飾屬
高價消費品，顧客都希望能夠買到質量有保證的產品，故此，顧客對品質標準和
工序質量均有非常嚴格的要求，期望銷售員能詳盡、仔細，有耐性地介紹產品，
加上近年愈來愈重視消費者權益，良好的顧客服務及為顧客提供專業珠寶知識往
往是致勝關鍵。

因此，本集團一向重視員工培訓，開辦不同類型培訓課程，如迎新班、產品知識、
顧客服務、店舖管理及服務管理工作坊、珠寶知識深造班等課程予員工，中國內
地員工更可登入「珠寶V課」流動應用程式透過個人智能手機接受培訓，旨以透過
專業進修，確保員工具備專業水平及應變能力，更能擴闊視野。

Occupational Health and Safety                                            職業健康及安全
Lukfook believes that occupational health and safety is not only          六福深信，職業健康及安全不僅為本集團的核
one of the core principles of the Group, but also our commitment          心原則之一，亦是我們對每一位僱員的承諾。
to every employee.

We comply with the Work Safety Law of the People’s Republic               我們遵照《中華人民共和國安全生產法》、
                                                                                            《中
of China, the Law of the People’s Republic of China on the                華人民共和國職業病防治法》及香港《職業安
Prevention and Control of Occupational Diseases and the                   全及健康條例》等法例法規，本集團位於廣州
Occupational Safety and Health Ordinance of Hong Kong and                 南沙的珠寶加工廠已成立安保部門，廠區全天
other laws and regulations. The Group’s jewellery processing              候均有安保人員巡查，本集團並會定期於各地
plant in Nansha, Guangzhou has established a security                     行政中心安排組織消防演習，及時防範╱消除
department, and the factory has security inspection throughout            安全事故隱患。本集團亦會為各地員工購買工
the day. The Group also regularly organises fire drills at local          傷保險，並為特殊崗位的員工配備口罩、手套

“Reduce, Reuse, Recycle and Renew” should be implemented               循環再造及更新使用」的4R原則。我們會在甄
throughout the life of the products we purchased. We also give         選過程中優先選用非過敏性、節能及耐用的材
priority to non-allergic, energy-saving and durable materials during   料。
the selection process.

Anti-corruption                                                        反貪污
We follow the Company Law of the People’s Republic of China,           我們遵照《中華人民共和國公司法》、《中華人
the Anti-Unfair Competition Law of the People’s Republic of China      民共和國反不正當競爭法》及香港《防止賄賂
and the Hong Kong Prevention of Bribery Ordinance and other            條例》等法例法規，向每位員工派發員工手冊
regulations, and distribute employee handbooks and notices             及不定期（如節日前夕）發佈的通告，清晰向每
issued irregularly (such as on the eve of the festival) to each        位員工說明其與供應商╱合作夥伴之間的有關
employee to clearly explain the policy regarding the acceptance        收受利益政策。本集團每當與交易方簽署合同
of benefits between them and suppliers/partners. Whenever the          時，均會附上本集團自行制訂的一套《廉潔交
Group signs a contract with the trading party, they are required       易政策》的要求予交易方簽署，當中詳列本集
to sign the attached set of Clean Trading Policy set by the Group      團廉潔守則、舉報方式等。
which details the Group’s integrity code and whistleblowing
methods.

Grievance Mechanism                                                    申訴機制
If employees encounter unequal treatment in their daily work           員工若在日常工作中遇到不平等待遇或發現其
or find other employees’ inappropriate behavior, they can              他員工不恰當的行為時，可通過電郵、電話或
directly appeal to the Group by email, telephone or face-to-face       面談直接向本集團提出申訴，本集團將予以保
conversation, and the Group will keep confidential to protect the      密，以保護舉報人員。
whistle-blower.

The board of directors (the “Board”) of Luk Fook Holdings         六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」，連同其
(International) Limited (the “Company”, together with its         附屬公司，統稱「本集團」）董事會（「董事會」）
subsidiaries, the “Group”) recognises that sound and effective    深明，在強調完整性、透明度、問責性及獨立性
corporate governance practices and procedures, with an            之情況下，穩健及有效之企業管治常規及程序
emphasis on integrity, transparency, accountability and           對提升股東價值及保障股東權益極為重要。本
independence, are essential to enhance the shareholders’ value    公司承諾維持良好企業管治標準，並致力確保
and safeguard the shareholders’ interest. The Company is          按照所有適用規則及規例進行業務。
committed to maintaining a good corporate governance standard
and endeavours to ensure that its businesses are conducted in
accordance with all applicable rules and regulations.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                        企業管治常規
The Company has applied the principles and complied with all          於截至2019年3月31日止年度，本公司已採用
code provisions and, where applicable, the recommended best           香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）證券上
practices of the Corporate Governance Code and Corporate              市規則（「上市規則」）附錄14所載之《企業管治
Governance Report (the “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix              守則》及《企業管治報告》  （「企業管治守則」）
14 to the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities (the “Listing     的原則，並已遵守所有守則條文及建議最佳常
Rules”) on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock        規（如適用） ，惟下列偏離者除外：
Exchange”) through the year ended 31 March 2019, except for
the following deviation:

Code Provision A.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman              守則條文第A.2.1條規定主席與行政總裁的角色
and chief executive should be separate and should not be              應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任，使董事會
performed by the same individual, so that there is a clear division   管理與本集團日常業務管理之職責清楚劃分，
of responsibilities for the management of the Board and the           以確保權力和授權之平衡。
day-to-day management of the Group’s business to ensure a
balance of power and authority.

In view of the increasing trend of business deriving from the         由於中國內地市場帶動業務增長及基於中國內
Mainland China market, it is believed that Mr. WONG Wai Sheung        地市場對「對等階級」之觀念，黃偉常先生作為
being the Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company         本公司主席兼行政總裁，可望於未來在中國內
will further enhance the business development of the Group in the     地進行商務磋商時，進一步提升本集團在中國
Mainland China market due to the norms in the Mainland China          內地市場的業務發展。此外，董事會成員亦包
market on “status parity” when future business negotiations are       括合資格專業人士及社會上其他顯赫、富經驗
conducted in Mainland China. Besides, members of the Board            之個別人士。董事會認為，於董事會轄下委員
also include qualified professionals and other prominent and          會及兩名副主席之協助下，現有董事會之組成
experienced individuals from our community. The Board considers       可確保權力和授權之平衡。儘管如此，董事會
that the existing Board composition, with the support of Board        將不時檢討此架構，並於適當時候，考慮將兩
Committees and two Deputy Chairmen, can ensure a balance              職分開。
of power and authority. The Board will nevertheless review this
structure from time to time and will consider the segregation of
the two roles at the appropriate time.

Save as disclosed above, none of the directors is aware of any        除上文披露者外，董事概不知悉有任何信息可
information that would reasonably indicate that the Company is        合理顯示本公司現時或於截至2019年3月31日
not, or was not for any part of the year ended 31 March 2019, in      止年度任何時間曾經違反企業管治守則。
compliance with the CG Code.

(iii) Board Responsibilities and Delegation                                             (iii) 董事會責任及權力的轉授
       The Board’s role is to provide entrepreneurial leadership of                            董事會之職責為以企業精神，肩負領導本
       the Company within a framework of prudent and effective                                 公司之角色，於審慎有效之企業架構內評
       controls which enables risks to be assessed and managed.                                估及管理風險。董事會監控業務，而日常
       The Board controls the business but delegates day-to-day                                業務運作則交由執行管理層負責。董事會
       responsibilities to the executive management. The Board                                 制訂本公司之策略目標、價值及標準，確
       sets the Company’s strategic aims, values and standards                                 保其成員瞭解及履行對股東及他人之責

Risk Management Framework                                                         風險管理架構
The Company’s risk management framework provides a                                本公司的風險管理架構為風險管理程序提供
systematic and disciplined approach to risk management                            系 統 化 及 規 範 化 的 方 法，而 有 關 程 序 內 嵌 於
process, which is embedded in the system of internal controls as                  內 部 監 控 制 度，是 企 業 管 治 中 不 可 或 缺 的 重
an integral part of corporate governance. The risk management                     要 一 環。透 過 風 險 管 理 架 構 前 瞻 性 地 識 別、
framework helps sustain business success, creates value                           應對及管理本集團內主要風險來保持業務成
for stakeholders and supports the Board in discharging its                        功，為 持 份 者 創 造 價 值 及 支 援 董 事 會 履 行 其
corporate governance responsibilities by proactively identifying,                 企業管治責任。風險管理架構符合Committee
addressing and managing key risks within the Group. The                           of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway
risk management framework is aligned with the Committee of                        Commission（「COSO 」）的 綜 合 框 架，以 達 致
Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway Commission (“COSO”)                      有效的內部監控，該綜合框架由5個部份分17項
integrated framework for effective internal controls, which                       原則組成，5個部份包括控制環境、風險評估、
comprises 17 principles under 5 components, including Control                     控制活動、訊息與溝通及監控活動。部門及管
Environment, Risk Assessment, Control Activities, Information                     理層作為風險負責人須直接承擔風險管理責任
and Communication and Monitoring Activities. Departments                          並向風險管理委員會匯報。
& Management as risk owners take direct risk management
responsibilities and reports to the Risk Management Committee.

INTERNAL AUDIT                                                          內部審核
The internal audit is an independent function undertaken by the         內部審核為內部審核小組負責的獨立工作，並
Internal Audit Team reporting administratively to the Company’s         分別向本公司管理層及審核委員會作出行政及
management and reporting functionally to the Audit Committee.           功能匯報。內部審核小組獲授權取得一切所需
The Internal Audit Team is authorised to obtain all information         信息，以完成內部審核工作。
necessary to accomplish internal audit work.

The Board has conducted an annual review to ensure the                  董事會已作年度檢討以確保本公司在會計、內
adequacy of resources, staff qualifications and experience,             部審核及財務匯報職能方面的資源、員工資歷
training programmes and budget of the Company’s accounting,             及經驗、培訓課程及有關預算均屬足夠。
internal audit and financial reporting functions.

A risk-based audit approach has been adopted by the Internal            內部審核小組採用風險主導審核方針，定期檢
Audit Team. Internal control reviews are conducted regularly to         討內部監控，密切監察業務或營運變動，定期
closely monitor business or operation changes. Regular audit            進行審核測試，以確保主要監控正常運作。內
tests are carried out to ensure key controls are in operation. The      部審核小組亦視乎需要進行其他項目及調查工
Internal Audit Team also conducts other projects and investigating      作。
work as required.

Any significant internal audit findings are discussed with respective   任何重大內部審核結果均與各有關部門主管予
department heads to have actions agreed and subsequently                以討論，議定行動並作出跟進，以確保維持滿
followed up, in order to ensure that satisfactory control is            意的監控。
maintained.

In each semi-annual work plan, manning levels of the Internal           於各半年工作計劃內，內部審核小組與審核委
Audit Team are agreed with the Audit Committee. A summary of            員會議定其人員配備水平，並每年兩次向審核
the internal audit activities and audit results are also submitted to   委員會提交內部審核活動概要及審核結果。
the Audit Committee twice a year.

Procedures for shareholders to convene a Special                        股東召開股東特別大會（「股東特別大會」）
General Meeting (“SGM”)                                                 之程序
Pursuant to Section 74 of the Bermuda Companies Act 1981,               根據百慕達《1981年公司法》第74條，於遞呈
shareholder(s) holding at the date of the deposit of the requisition    請求董事會召開股東特別大會的書面請求日期
not less than one-tenth of the paid-up capital of the Company           持有本公司已繳足股本不少於十分之一，並可
carrying the right of voting at general meetings of the Company         於本公司股東大會上投票之一名或多名股東將
shall have the right to submit a written requisition requiring an       有權遞交書面請求，以請求董事會召開股東特
SGM to be called by the Board. The written requisition (i) must         別大會。書面請求(i)須列明股東特別大會的目
state the purposes of the SGM; and (ii) must be signed by               的；及(ii)須由呈請人簽署並送交本公司之註冊
the requisitionists and deposited at the registered office of the       辦事處，並可由數份同樣格式的文件組成，而
Company, and may consist of several documents in like form,             每份由一名或多名呈請人簽署。
each signed by one or more requisitionists.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                         股息政策
The Dividend Policy sets out the principles and guidelines that the     股息政策列載本公司就宣派、派付或分發其純
Company intends to apply in relation to the declaration, payment        利予本公司股東作為股息時擬應用的原則及指
or distribution of its net profits as dividends to the shareholders     引。本公司擬分發全年淨利潤的40%至50%予
of the Company. The Company intends to distribute 40%-50%               股東作為股息，惟須視乎股息政策所載若干準
of its annual net profits as dividends to shareholders, having          則而定，包括本集團的財務業績；現金流狀況；
taken into consideration certain criteria set out in the Dividend       業務狀況及策略；未來營運及盈利；資本需求
Policy, including the Group’s financial results; cash flow situation;   及開支計劃；股東的利益；任何派付股息的限
business conditions and strategies; future operations and               制；及董事會可能視為相關的任何其他因素。
earnings; capital requirements and expenditure plans; interests of
shareholders; any restrictions on payment of dividends; and any
other factors that the Board may consider relevant.

principally engaged in the sourcing, designing, wholesaling,             金首飾及珠寶首飾產品之採購、設計、批發、
trademark licensing and retailing of a variety of gold and platinum      商標授權及零售業務。
jewellery and gem-set jewellery products.

management practices of the Group are sufficient to mitigate              充份而有效地減低業務及營運中的風險。董事
the risks present in our businesses and operations as efficiently         會將部份職責委派予各個部門。
and effectively as possible. The Board delegates some of such
responsibilities to various departments.

The Group’s financial position, operations, business and                  本集團的財務狀況、營運、業務及前景會受以
prospects may be affected by the following identified risks and           下已識別風險及不確定因素影響。本集團採用
uncertainties. The Group adopts risk management policies,                 風險管理政策、措施及監控系統，防範及控制
measures and monitoring systems to pre-empt and contain                   所面臨的相應已識別風險。
exposures associated with the identified risks.

Rent                                                                      租金
As a retailer, rent accounts for a considerable portion of the            作為零售商，租金佔本集團的開支比重不少，
Group’s expenses. Rental increment on retail shops directly               零售商舖的租金上升直接影響集團之盈利。我
affects the profit of the Group. The Group strikes a good balance         們在店舖擴張與增加盈利之間取得一個良好平
between shop expansion and profit increment. The Group strives            衡。本集團積極透過與業主商討減租、關閉盈
to lower rent via negotiations with landlords, close branches with        利能力較低的店舖，並於其他地段尋求合適位
low profitability and search for suitable locations in other areas, for   置開設新店。
new shops.

1   GENERAL INFORMATION                                               1   一般信息
    Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited (the “Company”)             六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」）於
    was incorporated in Bermuda on 3 September 1996 as a                  1996年9月3日在百慕達根據百慕達公司
    company with limited liability under the Companies Act of             法註冊成立為有限公司，其註冊辦事處
    Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Canon’s              地址為Canon’s Court, 22 Victoria Street,
    Court, 22 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM 12, Bermuda.                   Hamilton HM 12, Bermuda。

    The Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”)              本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主
    are principally engaged in the sourcing, designing,                   要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品
    wholesaling, trademark licensing and retailing of a variety of        之採購、設計、批發、商標授權及零售業
    gold and platinum jewellery, and gem-set jewellery products.          務。

    The Company’s shares were listed on the main board of                 本公司股份於1997年5月6日在香港聯合
    The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                   交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
    Exchange”) on 6 May 1997.

    These consolidated financial statements are presented in              除另有說明者外，此等綜合財務報表均以
    Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”), unless otherwise stated, and               港 元（「港 元」）呈 列，並 已 於2019年6月
    have been approved for issue by the board of directors on             27日獲董事會批准刊發。
    27 June 2019.

2   BASIS OF PREPARATION AND CHANGE IN                                2   編製基準及會計政策變動
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
    The principal accounting policies applied in the preparation          編製此等綜合財務報表時所應用之主要
    of these consolidated financial statements are set out                會計政策載於下文。除另有說明者外，此
    below. These policies have been consistently applied to all           等政策已於所有呈報年度貫徹應用。
    the years presented, unless otherwise stated.

    2.1 Basis of preparation                                              2.1 編製基準
          The consolidated financial statements of the Company                 本公司之綜合財務報表乃根據香
          have been prepared in accordance with Hong Kong                      港 財 務 報 告 準 則（「香 港 財 務 報 告
          Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRS”) and the                      準 則」）及 香 港《公 司 條 例》第622
          disclosure requirements of Hong Kong Companies                       章之披露規定編製。除若干金融資
          Ordinance Cap.622. The consolidated financial                        產、黃金借貸及金融負債按公允值
          statements have been prepared under the historical                   列 賬（ 如 附 註4所 解 釋）外，綜 合 財
          cost convention except that certain financial assets,                務報表按歷史成本慣例編製。
          gold loans, and financial liabilities are carried at fair
          values, as explained in Note 4.

          The preparation of consolidated financial statements                 編製與香港財務報告準則貫徹一致
          in conformity with HKFRS requires the use of                         之綜合財務報表需要使用若干關鍵
          certain critical accounting estimates. It also requires              會計估算，同時亦需要管理層在應
          management to exercise its judgement in the process                  用本集團會計政策時作出判斷。涉
          of applying the Group’s accounting policies. The areas               及高度判斷或高度複雜性的範疇，
          involving a higher degree of judgement or complexity,                或涉及對綜合財務報表屬重大假設
          or areas where assumptions and estimates are                         和估算的範疇，在附註5中披露。
          significant to the consolidated financial statements are
          disclosed in Note 5.

          The only exceptions are short-term and low-value                        金融負債均獲確認。唯一例外情況
          leases.                                                                 為短期及低價值租賃。

3     SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                  3   主要會計政策概要
      POLICIES
      3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                             3.1 合併原則及權益會計處理
          accounting
          (a)   Business combinations                                          (a)   業務合併
                The acquisition method of accounting is used                         本集團採用購買會計法將所
                to account for all business combinations,                            有業務合併入賬，不論是否已
                regardless of whether equity instruments or                          購買權益工具或其他資產。購
                other assets are acquired. The consideration                         買一間附屬公司轉讓的代價
                transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                      包括：
                comprises the:

                (i)     fair values of the assets transferred                        (i)     所轉讓資產的公允值

                (ii)    liabilities incurred to the former owners of                 (ii)    被收購業務之前擁有人
                        the acquired business                                                所產生負債

                (iii)   equity interests issued by the Group                         (iii)   本集團已發行股本權益

                (iv)    fair value of any asset or liability resulting               (iv)    或有代價安排所產生任

      3.5 Property, plant and equipment                                              3.5 物業、廠房及設備
          Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical                       物業、廠房及設備乃按歷史成本減
          cost less depreciation. Historical cost includes                             折舊列賬。歷史成本包括收購該等
          expenditure that is directly attributable to the                             項目直接應佔之開支。
          acquisition of the items.

          Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s                                 僅於與項目相關之日後經濟利益可

    3.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                             3.9 非金融資產之減值
        Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or         沒有確定可使用年期之無形資產或
        intangible assets not ready to use are not subject to            未達到可使用狀態的無形資產毋須
        amortisation and are tested annually for impairment.             攤 銷，並 每 年 進 行 減 值 檢 測。須 作
        Assets that are subject to amortisation are reviewed             攤銷之資產乃於發生任何事件或環
        for impairment whenever events or changes in                     境變化顯示其賬面值可能不能收回
        circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may              時作減值檢討。倘資產之賬面值超
        not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised             過其可收回金額，會就其差額確認
        for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount              減值虧損。資產之可收回金額為其
        exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable                  公允值減出售成本與其可使用價值
        amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs         中較高者。就評估減值而言，資產乃
        to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing          按最低水平之可獨立識別現金流量
        impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels              （現金產生單位）予以分類。出現減
        for which there are separately identifiable cash                 值之非金融資產（商譽除外）將於每
        flows (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets              個報告日期審閱是否有撥回減值之
        other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are              可能。
        reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at
        each reporting date.

      3.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                 3.12 抵銷金融工具
          Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net             當具有抵銷已確認金額之合法強制
          amount reported in the consolidated balance sheet                   執行權，且擬按淨額基準結算或同
          when there is a legally enforceable right to offset                 時變現資產和結算負債時，金融資
          the recognised amounts and there is an intention to                 產與負債會互相抵銷，並在綜合資
          settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle               產負債表報告其淨額。此項合法強
          the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable               制執行權利不得以未來事項作為條
          right must not be contingent on future events and                   件，且必須可於正常業務過程中及
          must be enforceable in the normal course of business                本公司或對手方違約、無力償還或
          and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy               破產時強制執行。本集團亦已經訂
          of the company or the counterparty. The Group has                   立不符合抵銷準則之安排，惟仍然
          also entered into arrangements that do not meet                     容許在若干情況下抵銷相關金額，
          the criteria for offsetting but still allow for the related         如破產或終止合約。
          amounts to be set off in certain circumstances, such
          as bankruptcy or the termination of a contract.

      3.13 Derivative financial instruments which do                        3.13 不符合資格作對沖會計處理之
           not qualify for hedge accounting                                      衍生金融工具
          Derivatives are initially recognised at fair value on               衍生工具初步按衍生工具合約訂立
          the date a derivative contract is entered into and are              日期之公允值確認，其後按其公允
          subsequently remeasured at their fair values. The                   值重新計量。確認所產生盈虧之方
          method of recognising the resulting gains or losses                 法取決於該衍生工具是否指定並符
          depends on whether the derivative is designated                     合資格作對沖工具，如指定為對沖
          and qualified as a hedging instrument, and if so, the               工具，則取決於其所對沖項目之性
          nature of the item being hedged. Since the derivative               質。由於本集團之衍生金融工具並
          financial instruments entered into by the Group do                  不符合資格作對沖會計處理，故任
          not qualify for hedge accounting, changes in fair value             何衍生金融工具之公允值變動須即
          of any derivative financial instruments are recognised              時於綜合損益表確認。
          immediately in the consolidated income statement.

      3.14 Trade and other receivables                                      3.14 貿易及其他應收賬項
          Trade receivables are amounts due from customers                    貿易應收賬項為於正常業務過程中

          other receivables is expected in one year or less (or in            於一年或少於一年（或在業務正常
          the normal operating cycle of the business if longer),              經營週期內的更長時間）內收回，則
          they are classified as current assets. If not, they are             被分類為流動資產。否則，在非流動
          presented as non-current assets.                                    資產中呈列。

          Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at             貿易及其他應收賬項初步以公允值
          fair value and subsequently measured at amortised                   確認，其後利用實際利率法按攤銷

          allowance for impairment.

          impairment policies.                                                閱附註3.11(a)。

    3.15 Cash and cash equivalents                                        3.15 現金及現金等價物
        In the consolidated statement of cash flows, cash                    於綜合現金流量表中，現金及現金
        and cash equivalents include cash in hand, deposits                  等價物包括手頭現金、銀行通知存
        held at call with banks, other short-term highly liquid              款、原定於三個月或以內到期之其
        investments with original maturities of three months or              他短期高流通量投資。
        less.

    3.16 Trade and other payables                                         3.16 貿易及其他應付賬項
        Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or                   貿易應付賬項是於日常業務過程中
        services that have been acquired in the ordinary                     從供應商購買商品或接受服務產生
        course of business from suppliers. Trade and other                   之付款責任。倘貿易及其他應付賬
        payable are classified as current liabilities if payment is          項將在一年或一年內（若更長則在
        due within one year or less (or in the normal operating              業務正常經營週期內）支付，則歸類
        cycle of the business if longer). If not, they are                   為流動負債。否則，在非流動負債中
        presented as non-current liabilities.                                呈列。

        Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair            貿易及其他應付賬項按公允值初步
        value and subsequently measured at amortised cost                    確認，其後以實際利率法按攤銷成
        using the effective interest method.                                 本計量。

    3.17 Borrowings                                                       3.17 貸款
        Borrowings are carried initially at fair value, net                  貸款初步按公允值（扣除所產生交
        of transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are                        易 成 本）確 認，其 後 按 攤 銷 成 本 列
        subsequently stated at amortised cost; any difference                賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖
        between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and                  回價值兩者間之任何差額，會以實
        the redemption value is recognised in the consolidated               際利率法於貸款期間內在綜合損益
        income statement over the period of the borrowings                   表中確認。
        using the effective interest method.

        Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are                為建立貸款融資所支付的費用，當
        recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the                   部分或所有融資很可能將被提取時
        extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility          確認為貸款的交易成本。於此情況
        will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred                下，該費用在提取貸款融資前予以
        until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is                   遞延。倘無證據表明部分或所有融
        no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the              資很可能將被提取，則該費用將作
        facility will be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a             為流動性服務的預付款項資本化，
        pre-payment for liquidity services and amortised over                並在融資相關之期限內攤銷。
        the period of the facility to which it relates.

                 •    Trade receivables                                               •   貿易應收賬項

                 The Group applies the HKFRS 9 simplified                             本集團應用香港財務報告準則
                 approach to measure expected credit losses                           第9號簡化方法計量預期信貸
                 which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance                        虧損，該方法就所有貿易應收
                 for all trade receivables.                                           賬項採用全期預期虧損撥備。

                 To measure expected credit losses, the Group                         為計量預期信貸虧損，本集團
                 categorises its trade receivables based in the                       根據客戶賬戶性質及攤佔信貸
                 nature of customer accounts and shared credit                        風險特徵，將其貿易應收賬項
                 risk characteristics.                                                分類。

                 The expected loss rates are based on the                             預期虧損率乃分別基於2019
                 payment profiles of sales over a period of 36                        年3月31日 或2018年4月1日
                 month before 31 March 2019 or 1 April 2018                           （首次採納香港財務報告準則
                 (date of initial adoption of HKFRS9) respectively                    第9號 之 日）前36個 月 期 間 之
                 and the corresponding historical credit losses                       銷售付款記錄及於有關期間內
                 experienced within the respective period. The                        已經歷之相應過往信貸虧損得
                 historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect                        出。過往虧損率乃經調整以反
                 current and forward-looking information on                           映影響客戶結算應收賬項能力
                 macro-economic factors affecting the ability of                      之宏觀經濟因素的當前及前瞻
                 the customers to settle the receivables.                             性信息。

4     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                            4   財務風險管理（續）
      (c)   Capital risk management                                        (c)   資本風險管理
            The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                  本集團管理資本的目標為保障本集
            safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going                 團持續運作的能力，從而為股東提
            concern in order to provide returns for shareholders                 供回報、為其他利益相關者提供利
            and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an               益以及保持理想的資本架構以減低
            optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.             資本成本。

            The Group manages the capital structure and makes                    本集團管理資本架構，並因應經濟
            adjustments to it in the light of changes in economic                環境的變動作出調整。為保持或調
            conditions. In order to maintain or adjust the capital               整資本架構，本集團可調整支付予
            structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                         股東的股息、發行新股或獲得新的
            payments to shareholders, issue of new shares or                     銀行貸款。
            obtain new bank borrowings.

            The Group also monitors capital on the basis of                      本集團亦以負債資產比率監察其
            gearing ratio. The gearing ratio is calculated as net                資本。負債資產比率是以淨債項╱
            debt/(cash) divided by total capital. Net debt/(cash)                （現金）除以總資本計算。淨債項╱
            is calculated as total borrowings (including bank                    （現金）是以貸款總額（包括銀行貸
            borrowings and gold loans) less cash and bank                        款及黃金借貸）減現金及銀行結餘
            balances. Total capital is calculated as “equity”, as                計算得出。總資本乃以「權益」（如
            shown in the consolidated balance sheet, plus net                    綜合資產負債表所示）加淨債項╱
            debt/(cash).                                                         （現金）計算得出。

            The table below analyses the Group’s capital structure               本集團於2019年3月31日之資本架
            as at 31 March 2019:                                                 構分析如下：

            Gold loans (Note 33)                     黃金借貸（附註33）                       450,889       311,283
            Less: Cash and bank balances             減：現金及銀行結餘
              (Note 26)                               （附註26）                       (2,086,522)   (2,097,867)

    Estimates and judgements used in preparing the financial             在編製財務報表時所採用之估計及判斷，

    expectations of future events that are believed to be                出評估。所得會計估算按其定義，甚少會
    reasonable under the circumstances. The resulting                    與相關實際結果相等。下文討論有重大風

    the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within the next
    financial year are set out below.

          less estimated costs of completion and selling                       開支後的數額。此等估計乃根據現
          expenses. These estimates are based on the current                   時市況及生產與出售類似性質產品
          market condition and the historical experience of                    的過往經驗而作出，並可因顧客喜
          manufacturing and selling products of similar nature.                好的轉變及競爭對手應對嚴峻的行
          It could change significantly as a result of changes in              業週期所作的行動而有重大差異。
          customer taste and competitor actions in response to                 管理層於各結算日重新評估此等估
          severe industry cycle. Management reassesses these                   計。
          estimates at each balance sheet date.

      The contributed surplus of the Company represents the              本公司之實繳盈餘指本公司為交換Luk
      difference between the nominal value of the shares issued          Fook Investment (B.V.I.) Limited全部已發
      by the Company in exchange for all the issued ordinary             行普通股而發行股份之面值與本公司於
      shares of Luk Fook Investment (B.V.I.) Limited and the value       1997年本集團重組期間所收購相關附屬
      of net assets of the underlying subsidiaries acquired by           公司之資產淨值兩者之差額。根據百慕達
      the Company during the Group’s reorganisation in 1997.             《1981年公司法》       （經修訂）        ，實繳盈餘
      Under the Companies Act 1981 of Bermuda (as amended),              可派發予股東，惟本公司自實繳盈餘派付
      the contributed surplus is distributable to the shareholders,      股息後，須能於負債到期時支付債務；或
      provided that the Company would, after the payment of              本公司之資產可變現值據此不會少於其
      dividends out of the contributed surplus, be able to pay           債務、其已發行股本及其股份溢價之總
      its liabilities as they become due; or the realisable value        和。就本集團層面而言，此實繳盈餘重新
      of the Company’s assets would thereby not be less than             分類為各相關附屬公司之儲備部分。
      the aggregate of its liabilities, its issued share capital and
      its share premium. At the Group level, the contributed
      surplus is reclassified into its component of reserves of the
      underlying subsidiaries.
